With a period of 9999 the bar is drawn as soon as the entry list binds the view holder — hence the "instant crash", repeated on every restart once the entry was saved in the vault (and the reported link to the duplicate issue #496).\n\n## Fix\n\n**`TotpProgressBar.java`** — do the per-second chunking in the *time* domain instead of the *progress* domain, so it never divides by a period-derived integer:\n\n- Find the whole-second boundary of the remaining time (`((millisTillRotation - 1) / 1000) * 1000`) and convert that to a progress value, rather than quantizing progress units by `maxProgress / period`.\n- The repost delay is now always in `[1, 1000]` ms by construction (the old formula could also yield a `0` ms delay and busy-spin the handler).\n- Guarded against `ANIMATOR_DURATION_SCALE == 0` (animations disabled), which previously produced `(int) Infinity` as the animation duration.\n- `setPeriod()` now rejects invalid periods via `TotpInfo.isPeriodValid()`, so a non-positive period can never reach the modulo/division math.\n\n**`TotpInfo.java`** — `getMillisTillNextRotation()` used `period * 1000` in `int` arithmetic before widening to `long`;
